● Persistent refund-sign-failure detection — done

  Implementation summary:

  Layer: swap_store.go
  Change: New RefundAttempts int field on Swap. Persisted across restarts; reset

    on successful refund.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Layer: refund_driver.go
  Change: New maxRefundAttempts field on RefundDriver + SetMaxRefundAttempts
    setter. DefaultMaxRefundAttempts = 5 (~75s of tolerance against intermittent

    cluster failures before declaring unrecoverable).
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Layer: refund_driver.go rollback()
  Change: Each rollback bumps RefundAttempts. When the counter hits
    maxRefundAttempts, the swap moves to SwapStatusFailed with an
    operator-actionable LastError ("Refund failed N times (max=M) — likely 
    upstream mpcd / RPC issue: ...; manual recovery required").
  terminal_failures
     counter bumps. Critical detail: below the ceiling, the rollback DOESN'T
    touch LastError / LastErrorAt — the broadcast driver overwrites with the
    canonical "insufficient funds" string, which shouldRefund matches on for the

    next refund tick. Without this, the refund cycle stalls in any environment
    where the broadcast driver isn't reliably overwriting.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Layer: refund_driver.go executeRefund()
  Change: Success path resets RefundAttempts to 0 — defensive against future
    re-use of the same swap ID.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Layer: main.go
  Change: New --refund-max-attempts flag (default 5). Logged in the "refund
    driver started" line.

  Tests added (refund_driver_test.go): 4 new cases on top of the prior pass + 1
  existing test updated:
  - MaxAttempts_RollbackIncrementsCounter — below-ceiling rollback bumps counter
  and leaves LastError untouched
  - MaxAttempts_HitsCeilingTerminalFails — at exactly N rollbacks, swap moves to
  Failed with the operator-actionable reason
  - MaxAttempts_ZeroDisablesCeiling — confirms --refund-max-attempts=0 preserves
  legacy retry-forever behavior
  - MaxAttempts_SuccessResets — successful refund clears RefundAttempts
  - Updated TestRefund_RollsBackOnBroadcastFailure to reflect the new "don't
  touch LastError below ceiling" semantics

  Full regression suite (cmd/bridge + internal/cosigners + internal/mchain)
  green.

  Live verification status: bridge boots with max_attempts:3 in the
  refund-driver-started log — the wiring works. The specific stuck swap
  (swap_5010a82142ef1391) is in refunding state, not broadcasting, so it
  bypasses both broadcast and refund drivers — it became orphaned at the prior
  bridge restart while a sign call was in flight. This is a separate gap from 
  what this fix addresses and is captured in REQUIREMENTS.md as a known
  follow-up: an "orphan refunding recovery" pass that detects swaps stuck in
  refunding with stale UpdatedAt and rolls them back to broadcasting so they
  re-enter the refund cycle.

  What this fix actually closes: for any future swap that goes through the
  refund cycle, the bridge can no longer loop forever on mpcd 504s. After N
  consecutive sign failures, the swap exits cleanly into SwapStatusFailed.
  Operators see refund_stats.terminal_failures as a monitoring signal.

  Want me to do the orphan-refunding recovery pass next? It's ~50 lines + tests
  and closes the last gap that prevented empirical verification.

      628 +// rollbackOrFail is the shared rollback path for signing fail
          +ures                                                          
      629 +// (PreSign + MPC sign). Increments Swap.SigningAttempts; belo
          +w the                                                         
      630 +// d.maxSigningAttempts ceiling, rolls the status back to     
      631 +// SwapStatusBridgeTransferPending so the next tick retries. A
          +t the                                                         
      632 +// ceiling, routes the swap to SwapStatusRefundPending with an
      633 +// operator-actionable LastError so the refund driver returns 
          +the                                                           
      634 +// user's deposit instead of looping forever — catches both   
      635 +// transient destination-RPC outages and terminal cases like a
      636 +// destination chain with no tx assembler (today's BTC / SOL /
          + TON                                                          
      637 +// gap surfaces this way).                                    
      638 +//                                                            
      639 +// `cause` is the underlying error for logging; `userMsg` is t
          +he                                                            
      640 +// short string written to Swap.LastError for retries (the SDK
          + shows                                                        
      641 +// this to the user). `stage` labels the failure for the ceili
          +ng-                                                           
      642 +// hit log message.                                           
      643 +func (d *SigningDriver) rollbackOrFail(ctx context.Context, sw
          +apID string, cause error, userMsg, stage string) {            
      644 +  var maxedOut bool                                           
      645 +  var attempts int                                            
      646 +  patched, _ := d.store.Patch(ctx, swapID, func(s *Swap) {    
      647 +    if s.Status != SwapStatusSigning {                        
      648 +      return                                                  
      649 +    }                                                         
      650 +    s.SigningAttempts++                                       
      651 +    attempts = s.SigningAttempts                              
      652 +    if d.maxSigningAttempts > 0 && s.SigningAttempts >= d.maxS
          +igningAttempts {                                              
      653 +      s.Status = SwapStatusRefundPending                      
      654 +      s.LastError = fmt.Sprintf(                              
      655 +        "Signing failed %d times at %s (max=%d) — moving to re
          +fund: %s",                                                    
      656 +        s.SigningAttempts, stage, d.maxSigningAttempts, truncS
          +igningErr(cause, 120),                                        
      657 +      )                                                       
      658 +      s.LastErrorAt = time.Now().UTC()                        
      659 +      maxedOut = true                                         
      660 +      return                                                  
      661 +    }                                                         
      662 +    s.Status = SwapStatusBridgeTransferPending                
      663 +    s.LastError = userMsg                                     
      664 +  })                                                          
      665 +  if maxedOut && patched != nil && patched.Status == SwapStatu
          +sRefundPending {                                              
      666 +    if d.logger != nil {                                      
      667 +      d.logger.Warn("signing maxed out → refund_pending",     
      668 +        "swap_id", swapID,                                    
      669 +        "stage", stage,                                       
      670 +        "attempts", attempts,                                 
      671 +        "max", d.maxSigningAttempts,                          
      672 +        "final_err", cause,                                   
      673 +      )                                                       
      674 +    }                                                         
      675 +  }                                                           
      676 +}                                                             
      677 +                                                              
      678 +// truncSigningErr formats an error for inclusion in a user-vi
          +sible                                                         
      679 +// LastError, with a length ceiling so we don't blow up the SD
          +K display                                                     
      680 +// or the swap-row JSON budget on the rare upstream error that
          + includes                                                     
      681 +// a megabyte of stack trace.                                 
      682 +func truncSigningErr(err error, n int) string {               
      683 +  if err == nil {

      835 +func TestSigning_MaxAttempts_HitsCeilingMovesToRefundPending(t
          + *testing.T) {                                                
      836 +  store := NewInMemoryStore()                                 
      837 +  signer := newFakeSigner()

● Persistent signing-failure detection — done

  The fix in 4 changes (mirroring the refund-driver hardening pattern exactly):

  File: swap_store.go
  Change: New SigningAttempts int field on Swap. Persisted across restarts;
  reset
    on successful sign.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  File: signing_driver.go
  Change: New maxSigningAttempts field on SigningDriver + SetMaxSigningAttempts
    setter + DefaultMaxSigningAttempts = 10. New shared rollbackOrFail helper
    replaces the two duplicate rollback blocks (PreSign failure + sign-ceremony
    failure) — bumps counter, then either rolls back to bridge_transfer_pending
    (below ceiling) or moves to SwapStatusRefundPending (at ceiling) with
    "Signing failed N times at  <stage> (max=M) — moving to refund: 
    <truncated_cause>". Success path resets the counter.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  File: main.go
  Change: New --signing-max-attempts flag (default 10). Logged in the "signing
    driver started" line.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  File: Tests
  Change: 4 new cases: AssemblerErrorIncrementsCounter,
    HitsCeilingMovesToRefundPending, ZeroDisablesCeiling, SuccessResets. Full
    regression green.

  Empirical proof from the live LUX → BTC stuck swap (swap_1721f44af16d6252):

  Stage: Status
  Before fix: bridge_transfer_pending (looping forever)
  After fix: bridge_transfer_pending → refund_pending → refunded
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Stage: signing_attempts
  Before fix: n/a (field didn't exist)
  After fix: 0 → 1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 (ceiling) → reset to 0 after refund completes
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Stage: Wall time to terminal state
  Before fix: Never reached one
  After fix: ~55 s (5 ticks × 10 s + refund tick)
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Stage: Log line at ceiling
  Before fix: n/a
  After fix: signing maxed out → refund_pending swap_id:… stage:PreSign 
    attempts:5 max:5 final_err:"txassembler: no config for network 
    BITCOIN_TESTNET"
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Stage: User outcome
  Before fix: Funds stuck
  After fix: Deposit returned

  What this composes with: the refund-driver persistent-failure ceiling I just
  added. The signing driver now hands off to refund at its ceiling; the refund
  driver applies its own ceiling on the refund leg. So the failure mode that was
  previously "swap stuck forever" now has two reliable escape valves:

  1. Destination-side failures (no tx assembler, RPC down, etc.) → signing
  ceiling → refund_pending → refund driver returns deposit → refunded.
  2. Refund-side failures (mpcd down, signature/address mismatch, etc.) → refund
  ceiling → failed with operator-actionable reason.

  REQUIREMENTS.md updated — §13.1 signing driver row reflects the hardening +
  the live empirical validation.

  The "Destination RPC unreachable while building tx — retrying" infinite loop
  you were seeing is closed. Any future swap that hits this path will trigger
  refund within signing_max_attempts × signing_interval (default ~100s).

  Want me to do the orphan-refunding recovery pass next (the remaining gap —
  swaps stuck at refunding after a bridge restart), or pick something else?

❯ yes do orphan-refunding recovery                                              

● Designing the orphan-refunding recovery. The trigger is a swap stuck in
  SwapStatusRefunding whose UpdatedAt is older than some threshold (well past
  any reasonable in-flight refund). The recovery rolls it back to
  SwapStatusRefundPending, bumps RefundAttempts, and lets the existing
  refund-pending path retry — composing cleanly with the persistent-failure
  ceiling I just shipped.

      469 +// isOrphanedRefunding reports whether a swap in SwapStatusRef
          +unding                                                        
      470 +// has been there long enough to be considered abandoned. Thre
          +shold                                                         
      471 +// compares against Swap.UpdatedAt — the store stamps it on ev
          +ery                                                           
      472 +// Patch, so a refund leg in normal progress (where every step
          + writes                                                       
      473 +// the swap) keeps UpdatedAt fresh and stays under the thresho
          +ld.                                                           
      474 +// Only swaps whose containing tick was killed mid-flow accumu
          +late                                                          
      475 +// stale UpdatedAt.                                           
      476 +func (d *RefundDriver) isOrphanedRefunding(sw *Swap, now time.
          +Time) bool {                                                  
      477 +  if sw.Status != SwapStatusRefunding {                       
      478 +    return false                                              
      479 +  }                                                           
      480 +  if sw.UpdatedAt.IsZero() {                                  
      481 +    // Legacy persisted state without UpdatedAt — treat as old
          +.                                                             
      482 +    // Better to recover loudly than leave a swap stuck foreve
          +r.                                                            
      483 +    return true                                               
      484 +  }                                                           
      485 +  return now.Sub(sw.UpdatedAt) >= d.orphanRefundingAfter      
      486 +}                                                             
      487 +                                                              
      488 +// recoverOrphan rolls an abandoned refunding swap back to    
      489 +// SwapStatusRefundPending so the refund-pending path on a sub
          +sequent                                                       
      490 +// tick can retry the flow. Bumps RefundAttempts so the persis
          +tent-                                                         
      491 +// failure ceiling still applies across crash/restart cycles —
          + an                                                           
      492 +// environment that crashes mid-refund repeatedly will eventua
          +lly flip                                                      
      493 +// the swap to SwapStatusFailed via the ceiling path, surfacin
          +g the                                                         
      494 +// problem for ops.                                           
      495 +//                                                            
      496 +// Idempotent: the inner Patch is gated on status == SwapStatu
          +sRefunding,                                                   
      497 +// so a parallel tick or already-recovered swap is a no-op.   
      498 +func (d *RefundDriver) recoverOrphan(ctx context.Context, sw *
          +Swap, now time.Time) {                                        
      499 +  stale := now.Sub(sw.UpdatedAt).Round(time.Second)           
      500 +  patched, err := d.store.Patch(ctx, sw.ID, func(s *Swap) {   
      501 +    if s.Status != SwapStatusRefunding {                      
      502 +      return                                                  
      503 +    }                                                         
      504 +    s.Status = SwapStatusRefundPending                        
      505 +    s.RefundAttempts++                                        
      506 +    // Surface the recovery on LastError so the SDK / operator
          + can                                                          
      507 +    // see what happened. Don't overwrite LastErrorAt — preser
          +ves                                                           
      508 +    // the original "stuck since" stamp from before the orphan
          +.                                                             
      509 +    s.LastError = fmt.Sprintf(                                
      510 +      "Recovered orphaned refund (stale for %s, attempt %d/%d)
          +: the prior refund leg was abandoned mid-flow",               
      511 +      stale, s.RefundAttempts, d.maxRefundAttempts,           
      512 +    )                                                         
      513 +  })                                                          
      514 +  if err != nil {                                             
      515 +    d.failures.Add(1)                                         
      516 +    if d.logger != nil {                                      
      517 +      d.logger.Warn("orphan recovery patch error",            
      518 +        "swap_id", sw.ID,                                     
      519 +        "err", err,                                           
      520 +      )                                                       
      521 +    }                                                         
      522 +    return                                                    
      523 +  }                                                           
      524 +  if patched == nil || patched.Status != SwapStatusRefundPendi
          +ng {                                                          
      525 +    // Lost the race or guard short-circuited.                
      526 +    return                                                    
      527 +  }                                                           
      528 +  d.orphansRecovered.Add(1)                                   
      529 +  if d.logger != nil {                                        
      530 +    d.logger.Warn("orphaned refunding swap recovered",        
      531 +      "swap_id", sw.ID,                                       
      532 +      "stale_for", stale,                                     
      533 +      "new_attempts", patched.RefundAttempts,                 
      534 +    )                                                         
      535 +  }                                                           
      536 +}                                                             
      537 +                                                              
      538  // failTerminal transitions a stuck-unrefundable swap to
      539  // SwapStatusFailed (terminal) with a clear LastError that poi
           nts the
      540  // operator at the recovery material on the swap row (signatur
           e,

      700 +func seedOrphanedRefundingSwap(t *testing.T, store *InMemorySt
          +ore, staleFor time.Duration, mutate func(*Swap)) *Swap {      
      701 +  t.Helper()                                                  
      702 +  realNow := store.now                                        
      703 +  pinned := realNow().Add(-staleFor)                          
      704 +  store.now = func() time.Time { return pinned }              
      705 +  t.Cleanup(func() { store.now = realNow })                   
      706 +

      708 +    Status:             SwapStatusRefunding,                  
      709 +    SourceNetwork:      "ETHEREUM_SEPOLIA",                   
      710 +    DestinationNetwork: "LUX_TESTNET",

  The swap that's been sitting frozen since the previous bridge restart is now
  back in active processing. If mpcd's sign keeps failing 3 times in a row, the
  persistent-failure ceiling will route it to SwapStatusFailed. If it succeeds,
  the swap completes as refunded. Either way — no more orphans.

          +tusFailed` with "likely upstream mpcd / RPC issue" reason — ca
          +tches the empirical wallet-rotation case where mpcd returns 50
          +4 on every sign because the rotated wallet's MPC session state
          + was lost. Counter resets on successful refund. Zero disables 
          +(legacy retry-forever). 10 unit tests total cover the new path
          +s. **Orphan recovery** (added 2026-05-29 in pass 3): new `--or
          +phan-refunding-after` flag (default 5 m) + new third path in `
          +tick()` that scans `SwapStatusRefunding` for swaps with stale 
          +`UpdatedAt` and rolls them back to `SwapStatusRefundPending` (
          +bumping `RefundAttempts` so the persistent-failure ceiling com
          +poses). Stats counter `orphans_recovered` in `refund_stats`. E
          +mpirically validated against the previously-stuck `swap_5010a8
          +2142ef1391` — recovered immediately on first tick of a restart
          +, re-entered the refund flow normally. Combined effect: a swap
          + killed mid-refund no longer needs operator intervention to re
          +sume. |                                                       
      409  | MPC dispatch | ✅ native | Calls `mpcd` directly over HTTP (
           `/keygen`, `/sign`) via `--mpc-url`. Per-swap deposit wallet (
           created lazily on first quote) plus a long-lived release walle
           t per destination network. No intermediate Node MPC service. |
      410  | Explorer / settings / auth | 🟡 proxied | Falls back to `app
           /server` via `--backend`. |
      411  | Storage durability | 🟡 in-memory | Default store is `swap_s
           tore.go` (process-local map). `zap_store.go` SQLite backing ex
           ists but is not the default — a binary restart drops in-flight
            swaps. |

● Orphan-refunding recovery — done

  The fix (refund_driver.go):

  Component: orphanRefundingAfter field on RefundDriver +
  SetOrphanRefundingAfter
    setter + DefaultOrphanRefundingAfter = 5*time.Minute
  Behavior: Threshold for "this swap was abandoned"
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Component: New isOrphanedRefunding(sw, now) bool
  Behavior: Status guard + UpdatedAt staleness check; zero UpdatedAt (legacy
    state) → always orphan
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Component: New recoverOrphan(ctx, sw, now)
  Behavior: Atomic Patch: status refunding → refund_pending, RefundAttempts++,
    operator-actionable LastError. Idempotent via status guard.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Component: Third path in tick()
  Behavior: Scans SwapStatusRefunding swaps, calls isOrphanedRefunding, then
    recoverOrphan. Skipped entirely when orphanRefundingAfter == 0.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Component: OrphansRecovered counter on RefundDriverStats
  Behavior: Surfaced via /health.refund_stats.orphans_recovered
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Component: main.go
  Behavior: New --orphan-refunding-after flag (default 5 m). Logged in the
    refund-driver-started line.

  Tests (5 new): RecoversAfterTimeout, LeavesRecentAlone, ZeroDisablesRecovery,
  LegacyZeroUpdatedAt_Recovered, BumpsAttemptsAcrossCrashCycles. Helper
  seedOrphanedRefundingSwap temporarily pins the store's now to seed a stale
  UpdatedAt. Full regression green.

  Empirical proof against the live orphan (swap_5010a82142ef1391, stuck since
  the prior bridge restart):

  Boot:          refund driver started ... orphan_after:30000
  First tick:    orphaned refunding swap recovered ... stale_for:6418000
  new_attempts:1
  Result:        status refund_pending → refunding (re-entered the normal refund
  flow)
  Counter:       refund_stats.orphans_recovered:1
  LastError:     "Recovered orphaned refund (stale for 1h46m58s, attempt 1/3):
  the prior refund leg was abandoned mid-flow"

  REQUIREMENTS.md updated — §13.1 refund-driver row now reads "✅ native —
  hardened 2026-05-29 (three passes)". The "Known follow-up: orphan refunding
  recovery" caveat I added after pass 2 is now closed.

  Three passes of resilience hardening — composite effect

  Looking at the bridge now, the full failure-handling story:

  Failure mode: Destination chain has no tx assembler (LUX → BTC etc.)
  Old behavior: Signing driver loops "Destination RPC unreachable" forever
  New behavior: After signing_max_attempts (default 10) → refund_pending →
    refunded
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Failure mode: Destination chain RPC briefly down
  Old behavior: Same loop, no recovery
  New behavior: Below ceiling: silent retry. Above ceiling: refund.
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Failure mode: Persistent mpcd /sign 504 (wallet rotation residue)
  Old behavior: Refund driver loops, oscillates refunding ↔ broadcasting forever
  New behavior: After refund_max_attempts (default 5) → SwapStatusFailed with
    operator-actionable reason
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Failure mode: Stuck broadcasting with Sender or DepositAddress empty
  Old behavior: Silently looped (refund driver bailed on missing-Sender check)
  New behavior: After grace window → SwapStatusFailed ("no auto-refund
  possible")
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Failure mode: Legacy persisted state with zero LastErrorAt
  Old behavior: Grace-window check bailed → silent forever
  New behavior: Treated as past-window → enters refund path
  ────────────────────────────────────────
  Failure mode: Bridge killed mid-refund (orphaned refunding state)
  Old behavior: No recovery path; swap frozen forever
  New behavior: Orphan-recovery on each tick rolls back to refund_pending

  Every previously-stuck failure mode now has a deterministic terminal state and
  operator-actionable signaling. The bridge can't silently lose track of a swap
  anymore.

  What's next? Now that the MPC + refund path is hardened end-to-end, options
  include: (a) live verification by deliberately exercising the new ceilings
  under production-like load, (b) the b-chain JSON-RPC wiring (Path A from my
  earlier proposal — connecting to the LP-333 signer-set methods), or (c)
  picking up whatever else is most useful from the doc.

✻ Churned for 6m 45s
